# Best of Marketing: Forget Influencers, Focus on Narrative

## Metadata
- Author: [[Jaskaran from The Social Juice]]
- Full Title: Best of Marketing: Forget Influencers, Focus on Narrative
- Category: #articles
- Summary: Marketing is shifting from influencer-focused strategies to building strong brand narratives that engage audiences year-round. Brands like Taco Bell and Apple are using storytelling and events to create lasting connections with Gen Z and other consumers. This approach leads to more authentic and memorable marketing than relying solely on influencers.

##### Why The Social Juice now carries classified advertising.

Classified advertising is the acid test of a publication’s pulling power. Advertisers can measure exactly the effectiveness of every publication in which they advertise. 

Which means that newsletters gaining in readership, and therefore response, tend to increase in classified volume, while those losing circulation and readership tend to find their classifieds slipping away.

> If not now, when will your brand book a classified ad? **Any agency or brand with [€165 can get an ad](https://substack.com/redirect/1e511836-e2cc-4956-a536-74983bbd6941?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) into this part of the newsletter…**

P.S. This was an ad for ads.

##### Taco Bell vs. McDonald’s: What’s the new marketing playbook for fast food brands?

There are two timelines of how fast food brands can launch products. Both are cost-effective in their own ways, and also kind of cringe. It doesn’t matter what you think of [McDonald’s CEO](https://substack.com/redirect/c05dd025-e83f-4a2c-b32b-07f64728926b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and his general vibe, the guy was creating content about [his products for month](https://substack.com/redirect/d32b909e-f020-4dba-bb0e-c16508733894?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)s, and one cringe moment boosted almost every single previous video. All of which got negative responses on the surface, but under the hood, you will find a McDonald’s fandom getting exposed to a new variety of products. The brand already has a ton of fans that post free content about [trying their products](https://substack.com/redirect/db99f135-0c8e-4beb-a788-0ddbe77ecd5e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) from different countries.

Before this CEO drama, McDonald’s had [a pretty viral one-off moment](https://substack.com/redirect/86e9c0c3-5db3-4bd9-9a35-70a6d987d527?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with the interview of their U.S. President, Joe Erlinger, going viral on Good Morning America’s TikTok channel. It was nothing special, but they had the format right, with a very high-level person announcing the product launch. [Mischief@NFA](https://substack.com/redirect/6be766f9-495d-40dd-a898-3aa4dd1d7de2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) has this [morning or evening show](https://substack.com/redirect/56a3de18-3f82-4715-9b42-8d973d77ba9b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) announcement strategy on lock for almost every product-focused campaign. It’s about using a more consistently familiar face and show to announce a new product in a healthier tone. It’s a comments-free zone and allows brands to control the narrative, and more.

On Twitter, McDonald’s [Senior Marketing Director Guillaume Huin](https://substack.com/redirect/6384c1c3-fcd1-4080-87be-a17257da3ec8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is kind of the human face of the corporate giant, which people love to roast. And he continues to add a fandom lens and backstory to every campaign the brand launches. As a narrative-control strategy that doesn’t cost much, McDonald’s uses their internal leadership and traditional media to showcase their excitement and efforts. Sometimes they f\*\*k up, but it’s a fast food company, most of what they care about is selling their stuff.

[![Inside Taco Bell's 2026 Live Mas Live With Demi Lovato & Benson Boone](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!ZI--!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F5ce76189-03be-4dde-96f7-4e7b0571024e_780x458.jpeg "Inside Taco Bell's 2026 Live Mas Live With Demi Lovato & Benson Boone")](https://substack.com/redirect/d9ed7357-af77-4492-8c14-6749044b8948?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)What happens when you don’t sell traditional fast food like burgers? That’s a problem for [Taco Bell](https://substack.com/redirect/9dbd089d-98d7-4d6c-a645-c69498656d4f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g). There is no one debating if burgers are fast food or not, and McDonald’s is very much synonymous with burgers, or what they call a product. But Taco Bell’s identity is always in crisis, a lot of people like it, but there are also a lot of people calling it out as bland and unoriginal, which has landed the brand in a weird space.

And they said, hell yeah, we want to be weird and since you can’t define where we stand in the category, we will act just like a tech brand. [Taco Bell’s Live Más Live 2026](https://substack.com/redirect/9dbd089d-98d7-4d6c-a645-c69498656d4f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) was a brilliant example of a brand knowing how different audiences treat them, combined with how a brand should treat creators.

First, the whole keynote format of the event is all about showing the absurdity and brand-side participation in fandoms, giving their core audiences a reason to defend the brand. Second, having someone like [Vince Staples](https://substack.com/redirect/375a110a-0ed4-475f-9773-2f95876156e8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) host the event was understanding the haters and highlighting the awkwardness of an event like that. **As I’ve said before, sometimes you have to create an external form of audience reaction within your content to help some people see themselves in that person on the stage/video.** [Vince did that](https://substack.com/redirect/f8549390-37da-4c8d-8152-57ffaff5adc2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for a lot of people watching the viral clips, he was the face of the “can you believe they did this?” reaction. Everyone else on stage like [Benson Boone](https://substack.com/redirect/afdc72bb-6b53-4dca-827b-304d0cd2683f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) was there to create that cringe, but very wholesome vibe. [You get this part, right?](https://substack.com/redirect/f93ce8bc-e782-49aa-98fd-7d826bade9e0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)

Now, the cost-effective thing about this event is that it creates incentives for invited creators to lower their fees and increases the chances for other creators to cover the event by becoming [a piece of content/attention itself](https://substack.com/redirect/f93ce8bc-e782-49aa-98fd-7d826bade9e0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) that is hard to ignore. Most of the time brands are asking creators to talk about the product as a side character, a regular food review is more reliant on the audience’s interest and trust in the author. 

With an event like this, the audience has a balanced interest in knowing what’s the noise around the product, making it a hero. The easiest way to get your influencer charges to more reasonable rates is to make your product unignorable on its own, and that’s why Taco Bell does the event, and why McDonald’s does Good Morning America and CEO bites.

> **As usual, that was a very long way to say, if your product has no narrative, everything from influencer marketing to social media ads is going to cost more.** Through brand activations and events, or even controversies, you have to build a narrative strong enough to get organic and paid influencers going around your brand. 

> For Taco Bell, people talk about their weird event all year, the story is looking pretty much the same for McDonald’s CEO, the digital footprint is set.

P.S. I honestly [don’t see the CEO vs. CEO beef](https://substack.com/redirect/79f1a8fb-a5f7-40c7-9219-a62000ed7fcd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) going very mainstream. It was a good laugh and a limited trend. Though I do see more “Chief XYZ Officer” roles being introduced, and brands will fight through these artificial roles. It’s PC vs. Mac again, or [should I say](https://substack.com/redirect/cbc0c673-94b1-4e83-8d42-a5bbddd49245?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Ozempic vs. other GLP-1s.

* [A global premiere](https://substack.com/redirect/f226d378-a152-4e9b-b06a-fa411367f59a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g): Young Miko brings the heat to [Gap’s spring 2026](https://substack.com/redirect/da36bb0a-1a13-4c3b-ab74-f8825afa15ef?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) campaign.

> 🛼 IPSOS ‘[Causes and Effectiveness](https://substack.com/redirect/d81c7fe2-6eb2-4640-b2cb-65b48eb3f61b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ report reveals 78% of Britons care deeply about at least one cause.

> 37% of Britons say they don’t care if brands are “ethical or socially responsible”. And a majority feel that the government, rather than private companies, should act for a cause.

* [DBS](https://substack.com/redirect/8c375e29-f9ec-4475-9c95-7468656073ee?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) looks to launch creative pitch.
* [Samsung](https://substack.com/redirect/4f1e2624-2009-4407-9fe2-48776637b030?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) appoints agency for CRM transformation task.
* American Airlines, Estée Lauder and Kroger are [among the brands](https://substack.com/redirect/510a3edb-76a6-4684-b18d-9da34d79f94f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) taking a look at their agency relationships.
* Scottish Power shortlists [four agencies](https://substack.com/redirect/fdbfb56d-d6ce-4aac-8055-74b179bf7668?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in media review.
* Tiffany & Co. adds [American Haiku](https://substack.com/redirect/13b09ee8-61b3-4c5d-a9f3-e4dbae966aae?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to agency roster.
* Zoom [names](https://substack.com/redirect/d7d7ab85-b85f-4dcb-90d1-cdac1f7082e9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) PMG global media AOR.
* Africa Creative takes lead on [Kimberly-Clark brands](https://substack.com/redirect/6a7773be-7133-49d6-b036-612452575e51?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in Brazil.
* [BrandsMart USA](https://substack.com/redirect/39d3adf0-48a5-4f49-bdd9-1b9526ef22f4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) appoints MEL as AOR.
* Tropicana appoints [Seen Connects](https://substack.com/redirect/6be3afbf-4e27-42c8-8fb0-b6b99a584fd4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to its social account.
* [Aviva and Direct Line](https://substack.com/redirect/f63da803-cb27-46bb-9463-717ca6f66bcc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) kick off review to consolidate media agencies.
* Care Bears taps [Ralph](https://substack.com/redirect/c2d53082-8e2b-4710-875b-befd8f3bcfe9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as social partner of record.
* [National Highways](https://substack.com/redirect/edb0b485-dd26-4d42-8acc-2ca5c3e19fa3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) shifts gear with ELVIS appointment.
* Net Conversion wins media duties for [Evermore Orlando Resort](https://substack.com/redirect/5b66c9ff-12b6-4771-871d-94f96a8ab5b9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Bupa UK appoints [Ace of Hearts](https://substack.com/redirect/b36e43c8-8e2e-4f9f-a6d5-623936970f7b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to handle its marketing and advertising account.
* Tesco [concludes](https://substack.com/redirect/4f36f228-4891-4ba5-8889-09bbb4e18353?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) closed media agency review.
* Riverford Organic appoints [independent media agency Anything is Possible](https://substack.com/redirect/f005dec6-3a8c-466a-a34b-439de2f34ab5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as its UK media agency.
* Aldi Australia [begins](https://substack.com/redirect/8a385099-b9ce-485b-96ab-63e18840f803?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) creative agency review.
* M&C Saatchi NA lands [U.S. Soccer Federation](https://substack.com/redirect/fed660c0-351b-42e1-a6de-d7e7c196b9a7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) integrated account
* 3Headed Monster [wins](https://substack.com/redirect/71aaaf79-7645-41a3-89d3-f3dceee5892b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Valvoline creative AOR.
* Stoughton Trailers [selects](https://substack.com/redirect/1b16f2b0-4ee1-4b8e-ae69-eb24353c66a1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) broadhead as first AOR.
* Formula E picks [UK agency](https://substack.com/redirect/f7ae2198-de68-4277-a593-963b45218e26?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to steer major CRM strategy.
* Noise Media wins [U.S. remit](https://substack.com/redirect/ddd7f671-8389-475f-bd3f-20a236d5478b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for Indian Motorcycle.
* Broadhead drives [Stoughton Trailers](https://substack.com/redirect/2ebdeaf8-091d-43ec-961e-070139f5b964?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as first AOR.
* Mizkan Europe picks [Beyond](https://substack.com/redirect/14a88149-85d9-40bb-a63c-73ebc2ddaa97?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to handle its creative and media accounts.
* Hardys Wine selects [Special London](https://substack.com/redirect/555feb01-038b-408d-8d89-e076b6763c9c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as its global creative agency

---

<div dir="auto" style="--image-offset-margin: -120px;padding: 32px 0 0 0;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><div dir="auto" style="text-align: initial;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;width: 100%;word-break: break-word;margin-bottom: 16px;font-family: 'SF Pro Display', -apple-system, system-ui, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Inter', 'Segoe UI', Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif, 'Apple Color Emoji', 'Segoe UI Emoji', 'Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: 400;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;margin-top: 0;">We’re eating good this week…it’s brand + advertising coverage from 01.03 — 14.03.2026….</p>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Before you go further, Attention is like money–you can waste it or invest it. If you invest it in my paid newsletter, you’ll get dividends for years.</p></blockquote>
<p data-attrs='{"url":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/bec55781","text":"The Social Juice+ (more of what matters)","action":null,"class":"button-wrapper"}' data-component-name="ButtonCreateButton" style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;text-align: center;cursor: pointer;border-radius: 4px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/16bf45c7-85da-4c99-863e-31264489d6d3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="font-family: system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';display: inline-block;box-sizing: border-box;border: none;font-size: 14px;line-height: 20px;font-weight: 600;margin: 0;opacity: 1;outline: none;white-space: nowrap;color: #ffffff !important;text-decoration: none !important;text-align: center;cursor: pointer;border-radius: 4px;background-color: #e85c57;padding: 12px 20px;height: auto;"><span style="color: #ffffff;text-decoration: none;">The Social Juice+ (more of what matters)</span></a></p>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="1100"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/509670d9-80dd-42eb-b3af-3f3619a2cac0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/01e1bfca-7b3a-4e58-80e6-f8bb4cb10eb2\_1100x220.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":220,"width":1100,"resizeWidth":null,"bytes":67342,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":false,"topImage":true,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/164226189?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F01e1bfca-7b3a-4e58-80e6-f8bb4cb10eb2\_1100x220.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="110" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!JnHP!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F01e1bfca-7b3a-4e58-80e6-f8bb4cb10eb2\_1100x220.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>El Segundo’s Beyond Meat drops “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f3b66d1b-0d45-4c08-9331-36b1245eb508?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Meat</a><span>” from its name, rebranding as </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/51addffc-5d83-4030-a158-3e8c5efdf7f2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Beyond</a><span>.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f998bed1-5c5f-422a-b9f6-2d71e0d897fd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Snapple</a><span> announces </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c580d0f6-119c-41df-a02a-06ddd1651a16?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">a visual refresh</a><span> and new marketing efforts, the brand is done with minimalism branding…</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Apple launches </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/037f0616-46ab-4485-9a59-cd785f799f90?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">new Instagram profile</a><span>, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/cff821e7-1c50-42b3-a128-840360b9c4e9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">finder icon mascot</a><span> and says “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8c790b52-3eac-47f9-be09-2c1144ff0c69?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">YES</a><span>” to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0ef49885-5d5c-48b7-9d78-c8201bec0ea5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Gen-Z marketing</a><span> for the launch of Macbook Neo…</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Apple hosts </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f94fa3e8-528d-4a92-bcbf-74b25bcc4b72?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">50th anniversary celebrations</a><span> around </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d150baa8-550f-4963-8b01-603c7195aea8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">the world</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meanwhile, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8c790b52-3eac-47f9-be09-2c1144ff0c69?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Samsung</a><span> partnered with a bunch of Gen Z and millennial influencers for the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bae848a4-edbb-42ba-a756-51d7edef59db?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">giveaway and launch of</a><span> the Samsung S26 Ultra.</span></p></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>UK consumer spending </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6e311f6e-b76b-4947-baf6-c66a7836383e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">slows in February</a><span> as inflation fears dim sentiment, survey shows.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Forget Influencers. Welcome to the World of the ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3fe61ad9-e14a-4d02-89f1-918f0a8cbf9b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Alternatively Influential</a><span>.’</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>New UK food ad rules “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/848da873-eadc-4bd3-9f8a-799cf684e1d0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">at risk of being a paper tiger.</a><span>”</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The new restrictions on the advertising of less healthy food to children may end up as a “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/34d2ae77-828c-4650-b3ee-a38b0e530ae9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">paper tiger</a><span>”, with as little as 1% of adspend affected by the policy once expected shifts to unregulated channels are taken into account.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b43d241d-08ba-4681-bde3-96107a521afd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Adobe CEO to step down</a><span> after 18 years at Silicon Valley giant.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>I’m a College Student. </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/dc25eece-edbb-4590-a4a4-f771edbf08b1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Gen Z Sports Betting</a><span> Is Wrecking My Friends’ Lives.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>BBC must remain</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4904927d-ed4d-4dbf-9049-a8aa65d4fad3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> ad-free and become more distinctive</a><span>, Radiocentre analysis says.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>
<h3 style="position: relative;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: bold;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 1em 0 0.625em 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 1.16em;font-size: 1.375em;">Why The Social Juice now carries classified advertising.</h3>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Classified advertising is the acid test of a publication’s pulling power. Advertisers can measure exactly the effectiveness of every publication in which they advertise. </p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Which means that newsletters gaining in readership, and therefore response, tend to increase in classified volume, while those losing circulation and readership tend to find their classifieds slipping away.</p>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>If not now, when will your brand book a classified ad? </span><strong><span>Any agency or brand with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1e511836-e2cc-4956-a536-74983bbd6941?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">€165 can get an ad</a><span> into this part of the newsletter…</span></strong></p></blockquote>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">P.S. This was an ad for ads. </p>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Report: Global investment scam network using </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/79d9c5d3-89a6-404b-8443-c4a39115e73c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Meta Ads</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>For </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c1e166b2-5a18-4db7-8265-a9c9b895b6e4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">many Black women</a><span>, the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4a356628-e66a-4d48-aad5-d3224b69e579?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Target boycott</a><span> is not over. It may never be..</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>With </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bd1df5ba-3aaa-497e-b556-0838f0b325e7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">$500M</a><span> in fresh funding, Quince’s valuation tops </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d44a1a31-0d27-4c4d-927b-c6804a44ab84?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">$10B</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Dear Mum…</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ae9e4743-1bac-4f19-8a4a-f4423b527404?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">1 in 8 Brits not buying Mother’s Day gifts</a><span> this year, new research reveals.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Lego posts </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c2c4e689-2842-46cb-8bd7-d84d37e3f91c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">record profit</a><span> despite geopolitical turmoil: </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e425ba24-f2a7-467b-9fad-7bbef6670acd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">CEO to AFP</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Can Gen Z </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7e1741ea-9419-45d4-8b5a-e4f1a672d115?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Save</a><span> Hollywood?</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/96df7746-866d-4fcb-867d-fcbe69e020ae?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">David protein bar founder</a><span> pushes back after </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/94be2ff5-a5a0-498d-92f7-98ded826ff24?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">lawsuit alleges</a><span> company undercounted calories.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>How </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5d314963-c44d-4192-93be-ecb8cd64f1b5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Swig</a><span> turned dirty soda into a national obsession.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta acquires </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6315c850-b2fc-4e9e-af0b-5d6b0eb8535b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Moltbook</a><span>, the social network for AI agents.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6d818b5e-43bf-43c8-9d58-8e855cafeb70?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Salaries of 60</a><span> New Yorkers.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Everyone at Fashion Week was </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/536fd766-f264-4da6-bd92-7aec542d6e3f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">wearing a Chanel jacket</a><span>.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>
<h3 style="position: relative;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: bold;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 1em 0 0.625em 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 1.16em;font-size: 1.375em;">Taco Bell vs. McDonald’s: What’s the new marketing playbook for fast food brands?</h3>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>There are two timelines of how fast food brands can launch products. Both are cost-effective in their own ways, and also kind of cringe. It doesn’t matter what you think of </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c05dd025-e83f-4a2c-b32b-07f64728926b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">McDonald’s CEO</a><span> and his general vibe, the guy was creating content about </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d32b909e-f020-4dba-bb0e-c16508733894?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">his products for month</a><span>s, and one cringe moment boosted almost every single previous video. All of which got negative responses on the surface, but under the hood, you will find a McDonald’s fandom getting exposed to a new variety of products. The brand already has a ton of fans that post free content about </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/db99f135-0c8e-4beb-a788-0ddbe77ecd5e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">trying their products</a><span> from different countries.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Before this CEO drama, McDonald’s had </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/86e9c0c3-5db3-4bd9-9a35-70a6d987d527?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">a pretty viral one-off moment </a><span>with the interview of their U.S. President, Joe Erlinger, going viral on Good Morning America’s TikTok channel. It was nothing special, but they had the format right, with a very high-level person announcing the product launch. </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6be766f9-495d-40dd-a898-3aa4dd1d7de2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Mischief@NFA </a><span>has this </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/56a3de18-3f82-4715-9b42-8d973d77ba9b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">morning or evening show</a><span> announcement strategy on lock for almost every product-focused campaign. It’s about using a more consistently familiar face and show to announce a new product in a healthier tone. It’s a comments-free zone and allows brands to control the narrative, and more.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>On Twitter, McDonald’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6384c1c3-fcd1-4080-87be-a17257da3ec8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Senior Marketing Director Guillaume Huin</a><span> is kind of the human face of the corporate giant, which people love to roast. And he continues to add a fandom lens and backstory to every campaign the brand launches. As a narrative-control strategy that doesn’t cost much, McDonald’s uses their internal leadership and traditional media to showcase their excitement and efforts. Sometimes they f\*\*k up, but it’s a fast food company, most of what they care about is selling their stuff.</span></p>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="699"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d9ed7357-af77-4492-8c14-6749044b8948?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="Inside Taco Bell's 2026 Live Mas Live With Demi Lovato &amp; Benson Boone" data-attrs="{&quot;src&quot;:&quot;https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/5ce76189-03be-4dde-96f7-4e7b0571024e\_780x458.jpeg&quot;,&quot;srcNoWatermark&quot;:null,&quot;fullscreen&quot;:null,&quot;imageSize&quot;:null,&quot;height&quot;:458,&quot;width&quot;:780,&quot;resizeWidth&quot;:699,&quot;bytes&quot;:null,&quot;alt&quot;:&quot;Inside Taco Bell's 2026 Live Mas Live With Demi Lovato &amp; Benson Boone&quot;,&quot;title&quot;:null,&quot;type&quot;:null,&quot;href&quot;:null,&quot;belowTheFold&quot;:true,&quot;topImage&quot;:false,&quot;internalRedirect&quot;:null,&quot;isProcessing&quot;:false,&quot;align&quot;:null,&quot;offset&quot;:false}" height="322.94871794871796" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!ZI--!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F5ce76189-03be-4dde-96f7-4e7b0571024e\_780x458.jpeg" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" title="Inside Taco Bell's 2026 Live Mas Live With Demi Lovato &amp; Benson Boone" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>What happens when you don’t sell traditional fast food like burgers? That’s a problem for </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9dbd089d-98d7-4d6c-a645-c69498656d4f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Taco Bell</a><span>. There is no one debating if burgers are fast food or not, and McDonald’s is very much synonymous with burgers, or what they call a product. But Taco Bell’s identity is always in crisis, a lot of people like it, but there are also a lot of people calling it out as bland and unoriginal, which has landed the brand in a weird space.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>And they said, hell yeah, we want to be weird and since you can’t define where we stand in the category, we will act just like a tech brand. </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9dbd089d-98d7-4d6c-a645-c69498656d4f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Taco Bell’s Live Más Live 2026</a><span> was a brilliant example of a brand knowing how different audiences treat them, combined with how a brand should treat creators.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>First, the whole keynote format of the event is all about showing the absurdity and brand-side participation in fandoms, giving their core audiences a reason to defend the brand. Second, having someone like </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/375a110a-0ed4-475f-9773-2f95876156e8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Vince Staples</a><span> host the event was understanding the haters and highlighting the awkwardness of an event like that. </span><strong>As I’ve said before, sometimes you have to create an external form of audience reaction within your content to help some people see themselves in that person on the stage/video.</strong><span> </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f8549390-37da-4c8d-8152-57ffaff5adc2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Vince did that</a><span> for a lot of people watching the viral clips, he was the face of the “can you believe they did this?” reaction. Everyone else on stage like </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/afdc72bb-6b53-4dca-827b-304d0cd2683f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Benson Boone</a><span> was there to create that cringe, but very wholesome vibe. </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f93ce8bc-e782-49aa-98fd-7d826bade9e0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">You get this part, right?</a></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Now, the cost-effective thing about this event is that it creates incentives for invited creators to lower their fees and increases the chances for other creators to cover the event by becoming </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f93ce8bc-e782-49aa-98fd-7d826bade9e0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">a piece of content/attention itself</a><span> that is hard to ignore. Most of the time brands are asking creators to talk about the product as a side character, a regular food review is more reliant on the audience’s interest and trust in the author. </span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">With an event like this, the audience has a balanced interest in knowing what’s the noise around the product, making it a hero. The easiest way to get your influencer charges to more reasonable rates is to make your product unignorable on its own, and that’s why Taco Bell does the event, and why McDonald’s does Good Morning America and CEO bites.</p>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><strong>As usual, that was a very long way to say, if your product has no narrative, everything from influencer marketing to social media ads is going to cost more.</strong><span> Through brand activations and events, or even controversies, you have to build a narrative strong enough to get organic and paid influencers going around your brand. </span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">For Taco Bell, people talk about their weird event all year, the story is looking pretty much the same for McDonald’s CEO, the digital footprint is set.</p>
</blockquote>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>P.S. I honestly </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/79f1a8fb-a5f7-40c7-9219-a62000ed7fcd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">don’t see the CEO vs. CEO beef</a><span> going very mainstream. It was a good laugh and a limited trend. Though I do see more “Chief XYZ Officer” roles being introduced, and brands will fight through these artificial roles. It’s PC vs. Mac again, or </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/cbc0c673-94b1-4e83-8d42-a5bbddd49245?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">should I say</a><span> Ozempic vs. other GLP-1s.</span></p>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>AMC Theatres and Cinemark are releasing </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/cd5b4212-2b3d-4127-b202-6c238d2fb14e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">a bright red handbag-shaped popcorn bucket</a><span> for the release of “Devil Wears Prada 2” on May 1.</span></p></li>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>It’s Mother’s Day weekend in the UK, and </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e18cb646-b498-48ed-9f6f-a66d2a3ea106?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">everyone</a><span> in the creative scene is </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1262f172-bfd2-48e4-97ba-269d3e31458d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">loving the copywriting</a><span> of this campaign from </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ba0fe0e8-2ebe-4cc1-80fd-597b9e83544b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Bloom &amp; Wild</a><span>.</span></p></li>
</ul>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Benjamin Moore </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c28a2e2c-055b-46a4-8e0d-7d3017953aef?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">paints a timeless picture</a><span> of growing up.</span></p></li></ul>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>New Target CEO </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8ab54ecc-4ea4-4803-95a7-bf8a637d9dcf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">slashes</a><span> prices. Previous cuts offered short-lived sales boost.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Pablo releases the next chapter for ALDI’s new brand platform ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ee5b8f83-cf79-4953-ae9c-640ce2abc8f3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">It’s Not Complicated.</a><span>”</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/305a18ce-af5a-4e15-a3b2-8826d8066b53?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">IKEA PH </a><span>launches Project Panchi Akap Pabalik </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bc1fbaf8-788d-46c9-a872-ac1e945ac764?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">to support</a><span> children, wildlife.</span></p>

</li>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;">Australians are engaging with Ad Standards in growing numbers, with 5,256 complaints lodged in 2025, a 26% increase on 2024.</p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/982be21c-35c5-4320-b9d4-b4e2553673e2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Barb Ads Hub</a><span> adds pre-campaign planning functionality.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta brings </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ed1b5a69-23aa-4f76-ac15-b60867247f6e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Agency Awards</a><span> to ANZ.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Heads &amp; Tales </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e031be04-ace2-4724-ae85-bf514f544756?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">acquires</a><span> Sydney design agency Holy Cow!</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Ogilvy Health introduces new offering for </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1ac60682-fc24-49a8-a908-ab4be17914c8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">OTC brands</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Horizon Media </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/286053b2-8fbe-49a5-81cc-42231a507e25?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">pilots AI solution</a><span> to detect cultural shifts mid-campaign.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>🛼 IPSOS ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d81c7fe2-6eb2-4640-b2cb-65b48eb3f61b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Causes and Effectiveness</a><span>’ report reveals 78% of Britons care deeply about at least one cause.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">37% of Britons say they don’t care if brands are “ethical or socially responsible”. And a majority feel that the government, rather than private companies, should act for a cause.</p>
</blockquote>
